- **second-order**: Reason past the obvious, first-order consequence to the second-, third-, and long-tail effects everyone else stops short of. Where most analysis says "X causes Y", this skill asks "and then what?" — mapping the cascade, surfacing the non-obvious winners and losers, the reflexive responses, and the effects that only show up over time. Use it on policy changes, product/pricing decisions, market events, technology shifts, regulations, and strategy calls. - **second-pass**: Adds the second pass to every coding task. After the agent writes code, it lists the odd cases the request implies, writes a check for each, runs those checks and the project's tests, fixes and reruns for up to three rounds, then reports what was checked, what passed and what was not checked. Fixes "the code worked on the normal case and broke on the first odd one." Use after writing or changing any code and before you report a coding task as finished. Also on /second-pass. ([nadavnaveh/skills](https://github.com/nadavnaveh/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/second-pass) · ★3)
- **second-pass**: Use when about to return a non-measurable output — writing, proposals, plans, explanations, designs, drafts, or any artifact whose quality is judged rather than tested. Also use when the user says "second pass this", "grade this", "is this good enough?", "self-judge", "iterate on this". Do NOT use for measurable outputs (passing tests, successful deploys, compile output, API 200s). Invoke before delivery to the user, not after. ([HubWizard/second-pass](https://github.com/HubWizard/second-pass/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
